Gradient of b:
For each lag k, ∂b_k/∂p_i = 2 p_{k-i} if valid. So gradient of weighted sum ∑ w_k b_k = 2∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. This can be computed by FFT: if we define w padded length m, P = fft(p,m), W=fft(w,m). Implement projection simplex:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    u=np.sort(v[::-1])
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,v.size+1))-css>0)[0][-1]
    thr=css[rho]/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-thr,0)
```

  normalize.

Let's detail local optimizer with both mirror and projected. We can combine them in one function that alternates methods.

Pseudo for `_local_search(p, seconds, seed, tag)`:
```
p0 = _norm_nonneg(p)
best_p=p0.copy(); best_s=score_fft(p0)
R=default_rng(seed)
method='mirror'
tau=0.08; lam=0.3; eta=0.6; lr=0.2
momentum=0; maybe
no_improve=0
last_improve=time
while time<deadline:
  progress = ...
  # anneal tau: start 0.12 -> 0.01
  # if no_improve > limit, perturb and maybe switch method
  if method mirror:
     improve, p, eta, tau = _mirror_step(p,best_s,best_p,eta,tau,lam,R,progress)
  else:
     improve, p, lr, tau = _projected_step(...)
  if new best: no_improve=0 else no_improve++
  if no_improve > 50:
     sigma=0.02+0.2*rand
     p=perturb log(p)
     # maybe reset no
  if time remaining? switch method every 30 iterations or when stuck.
return best_p,best_s
```

Let's design `_projection`:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,v.size+1))-css > 0)[0]
    if rho.size==0: return v/v.size? 
    thr=css[rho[-1]]/(rho[-1]+1)
    return np.maximum(v-thr,0)
```
Check formula: For sorted descending u, find largest rho where u_rho - (css_rho)/(rho+1) >0. Usually. Need safe.

`_normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.nan_to_num? clip 0; s=sum; if s<=0: uniform; else a/s
```

`_score_sequence_fft(seq, n=None)`: returns float. If seq length bad inf.

`_fft_b(p,m)`:
```
F=np.fft.fft(p,m); b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2n-1]; b=np.maximum(b,0)
```
`_corr_weights_p(w,p,m)`:
```
Fp=np.fft.fft(p,m); W=np.fft.fft(w,m); r=np.fft.ifft(W*np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]
```

In mirror step, if we compute F for b and then for grad, we also need Fp. Return from a combined function:
```
def _b_and_grad(p,w,m):
 n=p.size
 F=np.fft.fft(p,m)
 b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
 b=np.maximum(b,0)
 FW=np.fft.fft(w,m)
 r=np.fft.ifft(FW*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
 return b, 2*r, F
```
If w None maybe. But for score candidate, only b.

Could reduce cost by using same p in inner eta trials? No, candidate p differs, need new FFT. But we can calculate approximate directional derivative to choose eta? Maybe line search with few.

Time estimate: n=2048, m=4096. Each FFT 4096 complex ~0.05ms? Python maybe 0.1. 2-3 FFT per trial. If 5 trials * 20000 iter =200k FFT ~20s? ok.

But iteration loop in Python with many vector ops maybe 20000 iterations can be 5s? Good.

Need handle exact evaluation at end maybe `evaluate_sequence` with list. But we shouldn't call too many exact for 4096 due slow; maybe 10. We can call exact for top candidates at each stage.

Length strategy:
- Start with prev length 2048. We can also try length 1024, 2048, 32? Smaller maybe not lower but faster local to different basin. Since score tends to limit as n increases, optimum likely larger. But local at 2048 from prev. We can also refine at 4096 by upscaling prev (interpolation) and local. def _basis_matrix(n, nbasis):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  B=np.empty((n,nbasis))
  j=0
  B[:,j]=x-0.5; j+=1
  if j<nbasis: B[:,j]=(x-0.5)**2; j...
  up to degree 4
  then cos 2π k x, sin for k=1.. until nb
  if empty fill.
  # maybe high endpoint basis: x^5*(1-x)^5 etc
```

Weight formula:
```
def _active_weights(b, tau, top_frac=0.01, boost=1.0):
  bmax=b.max()
  if bmax<=0: return uniform
  x=(b-bmax)/bmax
  w=np.exp(x/tau)  # tau maybe 0.05
  k=max(1,int(top_frac*b.size))
  idx=np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
  w[idx] += boost
  w/=w.sum()
  return w
```
If tau too small, max weight ~1/k? For top uniform boost, okay. We can use `tau` as a fraction of maximum; smaller sharper.

For variance term, weight `b/bmax` normalized. But can be skipped.

    n = len(p); m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)

def _step_mirror(p, b, w, eta, tau, progress, R):
  grad = _grad_conv(p,w)
  # maybe add small variance
  scale = np.max(np.abs(grad)) + 1e-15
  # try eta values
  trials=[]
  base=eta*(1.0 - 0.5*progress)
  for e in [base, base*0.5, base*2, base*0.25]:
    e=max(1e-3,min(5.0,e*R.uniform(.8,1.2)?))
    arg=-e*grad/scale
    arg=np.clip(arg,-50,50)
    p2=p*np.exp(arg)
    s2=sum(p2); if finite: p2/=s2 else p2=p
    s2score=score_fft(p2)
    trials.append((s2score,e,p2))
  choose min score. if min<current -1e-12 accept and adjust eta according to e index; else if min<current? accept; else choose best but return no improve? Better accept plateau if min < current? Usually may not. If no improve, accept p_cur to allow eta exploration? But if min very close, no.
  # Adapt eta: if best trial e > current and improved, eta*=1.1; else if improved but smaller, eta*=0.9; else eta*=0.8.

Perturb log:
```
def _perturb_log(p, sigma, R):
  eps=1e-12/max(1,n)
  v=np.log(p+eps)
  # low-pass? just noise.
  v += sigma*R.normal(0,1,n)
  # optionally subtract mean
  v = v - np.mean(v)
  return _soft(v)
```

Perturb smooth:
```
def _perturb_smooth(p, amp, R, maxfreq):
  n=p.size; eps=max(1e-12/n, p.mean()*1e-9)
  v=np.log(p+eps)
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  h=np.zeros(n)
  kmax=max(1,int(maxfreq))
  for k in range(1,kmax+1):
    h += amp* (R.normal()*np.cos(2π k t)+R.normal()*np.sin(2π k t)) / (k**0.8)
  v+=h
  return _soft(v-v.mean())
```
No closures.

Top-level `_basis_matrix(n, nbasis)`:
```
def _basis_matrix(n, nbasis):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   cols=[]
   # zero-mean linear, quadratic, cubic
   c=x-0.5; cols.append(c)
   cols.append(c*c - 1/12)
   cols.append(c*c*c)
   # endpoint asymmetric? c^2*c? maybe
   cols.append(c*(abs(c)-0.25)) if?
   # trigonometric
   k=1
   while len(cols)<nbasis and k<=8:
      u=2*np.pi*k*x
      cols.append(np.cos(u)); cols.append(np.sin(u)); k+=1
   # pad
   B=np.column_stack(cols[:nbasis])
   if B.shape[1]<nbasis: pad zeros or random?
   return B
```
For n small, basis count maybe >n. Use min(n, nbasis).

`_basis_soft(n, theta, beta)`:
```
B=_basis_matrix(n, len(theta))
z=beta*(B@theta)
return _soft(z)
```
If theta length differs from basis count? Ensure.

```
lse = float(bmax) + soft_tau*np.log(np.sum(np.exp(x)))
val = 2*n*lse
```
- gradient wrt p: h=2*corr(w,p) (but if val=2n*lse, derivative weights same w and factor? derivative of lse = w_k ∂b_k, times 2n. So grad_p = 2n*h? But scaling of objective gradient matters for scipy. Use grad_p = h (drop 2n constant) or include. L-BFGS can handle scale; to be safe include 2n? It may be large. Use `grad_p = h` and val=2n*lse? Gradient scale mismatch. Let's return val without 2n for optimization? Since n fixed, minimizing lse same. Use `val = lse` and `grad_p = h` (derivative of lse w.r.t p is ∑w ∂b = h). Good. Actually derivative lse = w·b, derivative ∑w (∂b) = corr(w,p) times 2? Our h=2corr. Correct derivative of lse = 2corr. Good.
- Chain: `m_B = np.dot(p*grad_p, B?)` Let `h_i=grad_p[i]`. `t = B.T @ (p*h)` (sum_i B_ij p_i h_i). `u = B.T @ p`. `grad_theta = beta * (t - u * float(np.sum(p*h)))`.
Return `(val, grad_theta)`. Check: dp_i/dθ_j = β p_i (B_ij - ∑p_k B_kj); ∑h_i dp_i = β[∑h_i p_i B_ij - (∑h_i p_i)(∑p_k B_kj)]. Yes.

Implement:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
  n=v.size
  u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  css=np.cumsum(u)
  idx=np.arange(1,n+1)
  cond=(u - css/idx) >= -1e-12
  if not cond.any(): j=1
  else: j=int(np.max(np.nonzero(cond)[0])+1)
  theta=(css[j-1]-1.0)/j
  out=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
  s=out.sum()
  if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
  if abs(s-1)>1e-9: out /= s
  return out
```
For projection of p-lr*d, sum before projection 1-lr, after formula sum1.

`_step_mirror`:
```
def _step_mirror(p, b, w, eta, tau, top_frac, boost, progress, R, m, n, allow_plateau):
    cur = 2.0*n*float(np.max(b))
    grad = _grad_conv_m(p,w,m,n)
    scale = float(np.dot(grad,p))
    if not finite or scale<=1e-300:
       # random tiny move
       p2=_perturb_smooth(p,0.02,R,3)
       return p2, cur, False, eta, tau
    base = eta * max(0.2, 1.0-0.6*progress)
    best_sc=cur; best_p=p; best_e=None
    trial_factors=(1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25)
    p_floor = p + 1e-15
    p_floor /= p_floor.sum()
    for f in trial_factors:
        e=base*f*R.uniform(0.85,1.15)
        if e<=1e-6: e=1e-6
        arg = -e*grad/scale
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arg)):
            continue
        arg = np.clip(arg, -40.0, 40.0)
        p2 = p_floor * np.exp(arg)
        s2 = p2.sum()
        if s2<=0 or not finite: continue
        p2 /= s2
        sc2 = _score_fft_m(p2,m,n)
        if sc2 < best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_p=p2.copy(); best_e=e
    improved = best_sc < cur - 1e-12
    if not improved:
        if allow_plateau and best_sc <= cur + 1e-9:
            p_out=best_p; sc_out=best_sc
        else:
            p_out=p; sc_out=cur
    else: p_out=best_p; sc_out=best_sc
    # adapt
    if improved:
       if best_e is None: eta*=0.9
       else: eta = eta * float(best_e/base) ** 0.5
       eta = min(10.0,max(0.02,eta))
       tau = min(0.5,max(0.004,tau*0.985))
    else:
       eta = min(10.0,max(0.02,eta*0.75))
       tau = min(0.5,tau*1.08)
    return p_out, sc_out, improved, eta, tau
```
Note if trial has best_sc same cur (e=0?), best_e None. ok.

     e=base*f*R.uniform(...)

     q=_project_simplex(q)
     sc2=score
  improved=...
  adapt lr, return mom
```

def _simple_inits(n, count, seed, prev):

  res=[]

  t=i/(n-1) if n>1 else [0]
  for a,b in [(0,0),(0.5,0.5),(0.8,1.2),(1.2,0.8),(0.3,1.5),(1.5,0.3),(0.0,0.7),(0.7,0.0)]:

def _param_inits(n_target, npar, count, seed, max_seconds):
   out=[]
   R=default_rng(seed)
   nbasis=10
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_seconds
   betas=(0.7,1.2,2.0,3.0)
   attempts=0; i=0
   while len(out)<count and time<deadline:
      beta=betas[i%len]
      theta0=R.normal(0,0.8,nbasis)
      try:
         res=opt.minimize(_param_fun, theta0, args=(npar,nbasis,0.08,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':80,'maxls':20,'ftol':1e-8})
         p=_basis_shape(npar, res.x, beta)
         out.append(_interp_to_length(p,n_target))
      except Exception: pass
      attempts+=1; i+=1
      if attempts >= 20: break
   return out
```

Detailed code for local loop with dynamic hyperparameter adaptation:
```
def _local_search(p, seconds, seed, target_score, tag):
    p=_norm_nonneg(p)
    n=int(p.size)
    if n<3: return p, inf
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+float(max(1,seconds))
    R=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fft_m(p,m,n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
       p=np.ones(n)/n; best_p=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fft...
    p_cur=p.copy()
    eta=0.6; lr=0.30; tau=0.08; top_frac=0.02; boost=1.0; target_alpha=0.3
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    fail=0; it=0; last_improve=start; last_reset=start
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic(); elapsed=now-start; progress=min(1,elapsed/seconds)
       if fail>25 and now-last_improve > max(20,0.12*seconds):
          sigma=0.02+0.2*(1-progress)*R.random()
          p_cur=_perturb_smooth(best_p, sigma, 5, R)
          mom.fill(0); fail=0; last_improve=now; last_reset=now; continue
       aim = target_score
       if best_score < aim:
           aim = best_score*(0.995)
       target_t = aim/(2.0*n)
       b = _conv_full_fft_m(p_cur,m,n)
       cur_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(b))
       if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
           best_score=cur_score; best_p=p_cur.copy(); fail=0; last_improve=now
       w = _make_weights(b, tau, top_frac, boost, target_t, target_alpha)
       # method selection: prefer mirror first 2 cycles, projected when fail high
       method = ((it//25) % 2) if fail < 10 else (1 if fail%2 else 0)?
```
Simpler: maintain `phase` counter increment each 30; if phase even mirror else projected. If fail>15 force projected? Let's do:

Wait p index 1 is b; c2 derivative 2b. But expression r=2∑_k w_k p_{k-m}. For m=1: k=1 -> p0; k=2 -> p1. r1=2(w1 p0 + w2 p1). Good. Our ifft formula for i=1: ∑_j w_{1+j}p_j = w1 p0 + w2 p1. good. For p index0: i=0: w0p0 + w1p1. derivative: b0 2a; b1 2b; =>2(w0a+w1b). good.

Thus correct.

def _score_fft(p):
 p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
 if p.size<1: return inf
 p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return inf
 p=p/s
 n=p.size; m=_next_pow2(2*n-1); return _score_fft_m(p,m,n)
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / max(0.01, sum(a))^2

by combining FFT-based smooth-active-set local search, low-dimensional
parameterized initializations, and interpolation/refinement of the
provided previous construction.

The objective is scale-invariant, so all internal sequences are normalized
to sum to 1.  The final sequence returned is nonnegative and finite.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import scipy.optimize as opt


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _soft(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / float(x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _norm_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a.fill(1.0 / float(a.size))
    else:
        a /= s
    return a


def _conv_full_fft_m(p, m):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    F = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score_fft_m(p, m, n):
    b = _conv_full_fft_m(p, m)
    bm = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * bm)


def _score_fft(p):
    p = _norm_nonneg(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    return _score_fft_m(p, m, n)


def _grad_conv_m(p, w, m):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    Fp = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    Fw = np.fft.fft(w, m)
    r = np.fft.ifft(Fw * np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]
    return 2.0 * r


def _make_weights(b, tau_frac, top_frac, boost, target_t, target_alpha):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(b.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    bm = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    tau = max(1e-12, tau_frac * bm)
    x = (b - bm) / tau
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)

    k = int(max(1, int(round(top_frac * L))))
    if k > L:
        k = L
    if k == L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
    w[idx] += boost

    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        w /= ws

    if target_t is not None and target_t > 0.0:
        ex = np.maximum(0.0, b - target_t)
        se = float(np.sum(ex))
        if np.isfinite(se) and se > 1e-300:
            we = ex / se
            alpha = float(np.clip(target_alpha, 0.0, 1.0))
            w = (1.0 - alpha) * w + alpha * we
            ws2 = float(np.sum(w))
            if not np.isfinite(ws2) or ws2 <= 0.0:
                w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
            else:
                w /= ws2

    return w


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u - css / idx) >= -1e-12
    if cond.any():
        j = int(np.max(np.nonzero(cond)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        j = 1
    theta = (float(css[j - 1]) - 1.0) / float(j)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    elif abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-9:
        out /= s
    return out


def _perturb_smooth(p, amp, max_freq, rng):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    eps = max(1e-15, float(np.min(p)) * 1e-3, float(np.mean(p)) * 1e-10)
    v = np.log(np.maximum(p, eps))
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, max(1, int(max_freq)) + 1):
        amp2 = amp / (float(k) ** 0.7)
        ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        u1 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        u2 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        h += amp2 * (u1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph1)
                     + u2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph2))
    v += h
    return _soft(v)


def _step_mirror(p, b, w, eta, tau_frac, top_frac, boost, progress, rng, m, n, allow_plateau):
    cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))
    grad = _grad_conv_m(p, w, m)
    scale = float(np.dot(grad, p))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        p2 = _perturb_smooth(p, 0.02, 3, rng)
        sc2 = _score_fft_m(p2, m, n)
        return p2, sc2, (sc2 < cur - 1e-12), max(0.05, eta * 0.7), min(0.5, tau_frac * 1.2)

    base = eta * max(0.25, 1.0 - 0.6 * progress)
    p_floor = p + 1e-15
    sf = float(np.sum(p_floor))
    if sf <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sf):
        p_floor = p.copy()
    else:
        p_floor /= sf

    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0) if n > 3000 else (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25)
    best_sc = cur
    best_p = p
    best_e = None

    for f in factors:
        e = base * f * rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
        if e <= 1e-7:
            e = 1e-7
        if not np.isfinite(e):
            continue
        arg = -e * grad / scale
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arg)):
            continue
        arg = np.clip(arg, -40.0, 40.0)
        p2 = p_floor * np.exp(arg)
        s2 = float(np.sum(p2))
        if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1e-300:
            continue
        p2 /= s2
        sc2 = _score_fft_m(p2, m, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2
            best_e = e

    improved = bool(np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < cur - 1e-12)
    if improved:
        if best_e is None:
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.7)
        else:
            eta *= float(best_e / base) ** 0.5
        eta = float(np.clip(eta, 0.05, 12.0))
        tau_frac = float(np.clip(tau_frac * 0.985, 0.004, 0.50))
        return best_p, best_sc, True, eta, tau_frac

    if allow_plateau and np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc <= cur + 1e-9:
        return best_p, best_sc, False, float(np.clip(eta * 0.75, 0.05, 12.0)), float(np.clip(tau_frac * 1.08, 0.004, 0.50))

    return p, cur, False, float(np.clip(eta * 0.75, 0.05, 12.0)), float(np.clip(tau_frac * 1.08, 0.004, 0.50))


def _step_projected(p, b, w, lr, progress, rng, mom, m, n, allow_plateau):
    cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))
    grad = _grad_conv_m(p, w, m)
    scale = float(np.dot(grad, p))
    mom = np.array(mom, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if mom.size != n:
        mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        p2 = _perturb_smooth(p, 0.02, 3, rng)
        sc2 = _score_fft_m(p2, m, n)
        return p2, sc2, (sc2 < cur - 1e-12), max(0.05, lr * 0.7), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    d = grad / scale
    dm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
    if not np.isfinite(dm) or dm <= 1e-15:
        mom.fill(0.0)
        p2 = _perturb_smooth(p, 0.02, 3, rng)
        sc2 = _score_fft_m(p2, m, n)
        return p2, sc2, (sc2 < cur - 1e-12), max(0.05, lr * 0.7), mom
    d /= dm

    mom = 0.82 * mom + 0.18 * d
    momn = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mom * mom)))
    if not np.isfinite(momn) or momn <= 1e-15:
        mom = d.copy()
    else:
        mom /= momn

    base = lr * max(0.30, 1.0 - 0.5 * progress)
    factors = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0) if n > 3000 else (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25)
    best_sc = cur
    best_p = p
    best_e = None

    for f in factors:
        e = base * f * rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
        if e <= 1e-7:
            e = 1e-7
        if not np.isfinite(e):
            continue
        q = p - e * mom
        q = _project_simplex(q)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
            continue
        sc2 = _score_fft_m(q, m, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = q
            best_e = e

    improved = bool(np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < cur - 1e-12)
    if improved:
        if best_e is None:
            lr = max(0.05, lr * 0.7)
        else:
            lr *= float(best_e / base) ** 0.5
        lr = float(np.clip(lr, 0.05, 12.0))
        return best_p, best_sc, True, lr, mom

    if allow_plateau and np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc <= cur + 1e-9:
        return best_p, best_sc, False, float(np.clip(lr * 0.75, 0.05, 12.0)), mom

    return p, cur, False, float(np.clip(lr * 0.75, 0.05, 12.0)), mom


def _local_search(p, seconds, seed, target_score, tag=""):
    p = _norm_nonneg(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 3 or seconds <= 0.0:
        return p, _score_fft(p)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(max(0.5, seconds))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft_m(best_p, m, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        p = best_p.copy()
        best_score = _score_fft_m(best_p, m, n)

    p_cur = p.copy()
    eta = 0.65
    lr = 0.35
    tau_frac = 0.085
    top_frac = 0.018
    boost = 1.0
    target_alpha = 0.30
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last_improve = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = max(1e-9, now - start)
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, seconds))

        if fail > 25 and (now - last_improve) > max(25.0, 0.12 * seconds):
            sigma = 0.02 + 0.22 * (1.0 - progress) * rng.random()
            p_cur = _perturb_smooth(best_p, sigma, 6, rng)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            fail = 0
            last_improve = now
            continue

        aim = float(target_score)
        if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < aim:
            aim = max(1e-12, best_score * 0.995)
        target_t = aim / (2.0 * float(n))

        b = _conv_full_fft_m(p_cur, m)
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))
        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = p_cur.copy()
            fail = 0
            last_improve = now

        w = _make_weights(b, tau_frac, top_frac, boost, target_t, target_alpha)
        allow_plateau = fail > 7

        if (it % 30) < 22:
            p_new, sc_new, improved, eta, tau_frac = _step_mirror(
                p_cur, b, w, eta, tau_frac, top_frac, boost,
                progress, rng, m, n, allow_plateau)
        else:
            p_new, sc_new, improved, lr, mom = _step_projected(
                p_cur, b, w, lr, progress, rng, mom, m, n, allow_plateau)

        if improved:
            p_cur = p_new
            fail = 0
            last_improve = now
            top_frac = max(0.005, top_frac * 0.97)
            boost = max(0.35, boost * 0.99)
        else:
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur_score + 1e-8 and allow_plateau:
                p_cur = p_new
            fail += 1
            top_frac = min(0.12, top_frac * 1.04)
            boost = min(3.0, boost * 1.03)
            if fail % 12 == 0:
                sigma = 0.01 + 0.09 * rng.random()
                p_cur = _perturb_smooth(p_cur, sigma, 4, rng)
                mom.fill(0.0)

        it += 1

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _interp_to_length(p, n):
    p = _norm_nonneg(p)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if p.size == n:
        return p
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _norm_nonneg(q)


def _simple_inits(n, count, seed, prev):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = []

    if prev is not None:
        out.append(_interp_to_length(prev, n))
        out.append(_interp_to_length(prev[::-1], n))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = (i + 0.5) / float(n)
    right = 1.0 - left
    eps = 1.0 / float(n + 1.0)

    beta_pairs = (
        (0.0, 0.0),
        (0.25, 0.25),
        (0.5, 0.5),
        (0.35, 0.90),
        (0.90, 0.35),
        (0.20, 1.40),
        (1.40, 0.20),
        (0.55, 1.10),
        (1.10, 0.55),
        (0.0, 0.80),
        (0.80, 0.0),
    )
    for a, b in beta_pairs:
        l = left + eps
        r = right + eps
        if a == 0.0:
            val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (r ** b) if b != 0.0 else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            val = l ** a
            if b != 0.0:
                val = val * (r ** b)
        p = _norm_nonneg(val)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            out.append(p)

    for phi in np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 10, endpoint=False):
        for c in (0.35, 0.65, 0.90, 1.00):
            val = 1.0 + c * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (t - phi))
            val = np.maximum(val, eps)
            p = _norm_nonneg(val)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                out.append(p)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(count))):
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 9):
            amp = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) / (float(k) ** 0.65)
            amp2 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) / (float(k) ** 0.65)
            ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph1)
            v += amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph2)
        scale = rng.uniform(0.35, 1.40)
        p = _soft(scale * v)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            out.append(p)

    return out


def _basis_matrix(n, nbasis):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    nbasis = int(max(1, nbasis))
    if nbasis > n:
        nbasis = n
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = x - 0.5
    cols = []
    cols.append(c)
    cols.append(c * c - 1.0 / 12.0)
    cols.append(c * c * c)
    cols.append(c * (np.absolute(c) - 0.25))

    k = 1
    while len(cols) < nbasis and k <= 8:
        u = 2.0 * np.pi * k * x
        cols.append(np.cos(u))
        cols.append(np.sin(u))
        k += 1

    while len(cols) < nbasis:
        cols.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
    B = np.column_stack(cols[:nbasis]).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    return B


def _basis_shape(n, theta, beta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    B = _basis_matrix(n, theta.size)
    z = float(beta) * (B @ theta)
    return _soft(z)


def _param_fun(theta, n, nbasis, tau_frac, beta):
    try:
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if theta.size != nbasis:
            if theta.size < nbasis:
                tmp = np.zeros(nbasis, dtype=np.float64)
                tmp[:theta.size] = theta
                theta = tmp
            else:
                theta = theta[:nbasis]

        B = _basis_matrix(n, nbasis)
        z = float(beta) * (B @ theta)
        p = _soft(z)
        if p.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            return 1e12, np.zeros(nbasis, dtype=np.float64)

        m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        b = _conv_full_fft_m(p, m)
        bm = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 1e-300:
            return 1e12, np.zeros(nbasis, dtype=np.float64)

        tau = max(1e-12, float(tau_frac) * bm)
        x = (b - bm) / tau
        x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(x)
        se = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
            return 1e12, np.zeros(nbasis, dtype=np.float64)
        w = e / se

        lse = bm + tau * np.log(se)
        grad_p = _grad_conv_m(p, w, m)

        hp = p * grad_p
        t = B @ hp
        u = B @ p
        shp = float(np.sum(hp))
        grad_theta = float(beta) * (t - u * shp)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
            grad_theta = np.zeros(nbasis, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.isfinite(lse):
            return 1e12, grad_theta
        return float(lse), grad_theta
    except Exception:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(int(nbasis), dtype=np.float64)


def _param_inits(n_target, npar, count, seed, max_seconds):
    n_target = int(max(3, n_target))
    npar = int(max(32, min(npar, 512)))
    count = int(max(1, count))
    max_seconds = float(max(0.0, max_seconds))
    if max_seconds <= 0.0:
        return []

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = []
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    betas = (0.65, 1.0, 1.5, 2.2, 3.0)
    nbasis = min(10, npar)
    attempts = 0
    bi = 0

    while len(out) < count and attempts < 24 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        beta = betas[bi % len(betas)]
        theta0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.75, nbasis)
        try:
            res = opt.minimize(
                _param_fun,
                theta0,
                args=(npar, nbasis, 0.08, beta),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={"maxiter": 70, "maxls": 20, "ftol": 1e-8, "gtol": 1e-7}
            )
            p = _basis_shape(npar, res.x, beta)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                out.append(_interp_to_length(p, n_target))
        except Exception:
            pass
        attempts += 1
        bi += 1

    return out


def _load_prev():
    try:
        cand = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / s


def _consider_candidate(p, best_p, best_score):
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = _norm_nonneg(p)
    if p.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return best_p, best_score
    sc = _score_fft(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        best_p, best_score = p, sc
        return best_p, best_score
    if best_p is None or sc < best_score:
        return p.copy(), float(sc)
    return best_p, best_score


def _exact_score(p):
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    p = _norm_nonneg(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in p]
        sc = evaluate_sequence(vals)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _update_exact(p, best_exact, best_exact_p):
    if p is None:
        return best_exact, best_exact_p
    p = _norm_nonneg(p)
    if p.size == 0 or p.size > 8192:
        return best_exact, best_exact_p
    sc = _exact_score(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and (not np.isfinite(best_exact) or sc < best_exact):
        return float(sc), p.copy()
    return best_exact, best_exact_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point invoked by the evaluator.

    Returns a list of nonnegative heights that minimizes the evaluator.
    """
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    hard_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = budget_s

    if total_budget <= 120.0:
        opt_deadline = hard_start + max(30.0, total_budget - 8.0)
    else:
        opt_deadline = hard_start + total_budget - 90.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    target_score = 1.5027

    best_p = None
    best_fft = float("inf")
    best2048 = None
    best2048_sc = float("inf")
    best4096 = None
    best4096_sc = float("inf")

    prev = _load_prev()
    if prev is not None:
        p_prev = _interp_to_length(prev, 2048)
        sc_prev = _score_fft(p_prev)
        print("previous interpolated n=2048 fft score=%.8f" % sc_prev)
        best_p, best_fft = _consider_candidate(p_prev, best_p, best_fft)
        best2048, best2048_sc = _consider_candidate(p_prev, best2048, best2048_sc)

    if best_p is None:
        uniform = np.full(2048, 1.0 / 2048.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_p = uniform.copy()
        best_fft = _score_fft(uniform)
        print("no previous sequence, starting uniform score=%.8f" % best_fft)

    total_opt = max(60.0, opt_deadline - hard_start)
    n_main = 2048
    inits_budget = 0.55 * total_opt
    four_budget = 0.25 * total_opt
    final_budget = 0.20 * total_opt

    inits_end = hard_start + inits_budget

    param_deadline = min(inits_end, hard_start + 80.0)
    param_inits = []
    if time.monotonic() < param_deadline - 5.0:
        param_inits = _param_inits(
            n_main, 256, 5, seed + 101,
            max_seconds=max(0.0, param_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if param_inits:
            print("parameter inits generated: %d" % len(param_inits))

    n_init_runs = 5
    raw_inits = _simple_inits(n_main, 8, seed + 7, prev)
    for p in param_inits:
        raw_inits.append(p)

    scored = []
    for p in raw_inits:
        p = _norm_nonneg(p)
        if p.size != n_main or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            continue
        sc = _score_fft(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, p))
    scored.sort(key=lambda pa: pa[0])

    chosen = []
    seen = []
    for sc, p in scored:
        if len(chosen) >= n_init_runs:
            break
        diverse = True
        for q in seen:
            if q.size == p.size and np.linalg.norm(p - q) < 0.045:
                diverse = False
                break
        if diverse:
            chosen.append(p)
            seen.append(p)
    if not chosen:
        chosen.append(_norm_nonneg(best_p))

    weights = np.array([0.44, 0.22, 0.15, 0.10, 0.09], dtype=np.float64)
    weights = weights[:len(chosen)]
    wsum = float(weights.sum())
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        weights = np.ones(len(chosen), dtype=np.float64)
        wsum = float(weights.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0:
            wsum = 1.0

    for i, p_init in enumerate(chosen):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= inits_end:
            break
        rem = inits_end - now
        alloc = float(max(20.0, rem * weights[i] / wsum))
        alloc = min(alloc, max(10.0, rem - 2.0))
        if alloc <= 5.0:
            break
        print("init run %d n=%d initial=%.7f alloc=%.1fs" %
              (i, p_init.size, _score_fft(p_init), alloc))
        p_opt, sc_opt = _local_search(
            p_init, alloc, seed + 1000 + 13 * i + int(rng.integers(0, 1000)),
            target_score, tag="init%d" % i)
        best_p, best_fft = _consider_candidate(p_opt, best_p, best_fft)
        best2048, best2048_sc = _consider_candidate(p_opt, best2048, best2048_sc)
        print("init run %d done best_fft=%.8f current run=%.8f" % (i, best_fft, sc_opt))

    four_end = hard_start + inits_budget + four_budget
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 30.0 < opt_deadline and best_p is not None:
        p4096 = _interp_to_length(best_p, 4096)
        p4096, sc4 = _local_search(
            p4096, max(10.0, min(four_budget, four_end - now)),
            seed + 2501, target_score, tag="n4096")
        best_p, best_fft = _consider_candidate(p4096, best_p, best_fft)
        best4096, best4096_sc = _consider_candidate(p4096, best4096, best4096_sc)
        print("after n=4096 refinement best_fft=%.8f" % best_fft)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 20.0 < opt_deadline and best_p is not None:
        rem_final = opt_deadline - now
        alloc_final = min(final_budget, rem_final - 2.0)
        if alloc_final > 20.0:
            p_final, sc_final = _local_search(
                best_p, alloc_final, seed + 4501, target_score, tag="final_polish")
            best_p, best_fft = _consider_candidate(p_final, best_p, best_fft)
            best2048, best2048_sc = _consider_candidate(p_final, best2048, best2048_sc)
            best4096, best4096_sc = _consider_candidate(p_final, best4096, best4096_sc)
            print("after final polish best_fft=%.8f" % best_fft)

    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_exact_p = None

    for cand in (best2048, best4096, best_p):
        if time.monotonic() > hard_start + total_budget - 12.0:
            break
        if cand is None:
            continue
        best_exact, best_exact_p = _update_exact(cand, best_exact, best_exact_p)

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_exact_p = best_p
    if best_exact_p is None:
        best_exact_p = np.full(2048, 1.0 / 2048.0, dtype=np.float64)

    best_exact_p = _norm_nonneg(best_exact_p)
    out = [float(x) for x in best_exact_p]
    try:
        final_exact = evaluate_sequence(out)
        print("final exact evaluate_sequence=%.8f n=%d target=1.5027" % (float(final_exact), len(out)))
    except Exception as exc:
        print("final exact evaluation unavailable: %s" % exc)

    return out
```
